About this record

241 proposed statements and 246 proposed connections reconstructed by the AI from a public forum thread, essays shared in it and two supplied analyses. Each is credited to the posts or essays it draws on, with links. None of the people credited has reviewed or endorsed this map. Source locators, assumptions and unfinished work are published; actions name a proposed role but nobody is assigned, and no trial commitment or outcome is recorded.

Document representations, not original covers. The discussion, shared essays and supplied analyses play different roles; source descriptions distinguish them.

This is a proposed AI reconstruction from a public forum thread, 5 essays shared in it and the supplied analyses of it. Every item credits the posts or essays it draws on, with links, and names the analysis page or section it comes from and whether a connection is an inference. The people credited have not reviewed or endorsed this selection.

01What Would Make a Second Renaissance Possible?Explore+

What Would Make a Second Renaissance Possible? · Second Renaissance Forum · posts 1–36 and 38, 16–22 October 2026

The public forum thread this map draws on, opened by Leif Eriksson. Its first 36 posts were checked against the public topic on 22 October 2026, and post 38, Leif Eriksson’s response to an earlier version of this map, against a print of the topic on 23 October 2026. Each credit links to the post it names. The paraphrases are the AI’s; a credit says where an idea comes from, not that its author agrees with the wording, and none of the people credited has reviewed it.

08Second Renaissance threadExplore+

Second Renaissance thread: thinking-process trees · supplied text · 22 October 2026

Supplied earlier analysis and presentation notes, based on a reported 32-post version of the thread. Its throughput and governance-capacity proposals are hypotheses for a bounded case. The later analysis broadens the programme and distinguishes local improvements from a durable change in institutional selection. The draft introductory forum post it contains was not sent.

Cited in this release by 2 statements and 0 connections. Citation counts do not measure independent evidence or endorsement.

Follow this source into the reasoning

  • Accessible AI-supported conflict training and guidance: voluntary, confidential, with human support. →Injections I1–I3 and negative branches 4–5 · AI reconstruction
  • Apparent resolution conceals domination: grievances are suppressed while satisfaction rises. →Negative branch 4, Domesticated conflict · AI reconstruction

Showing 2 examples; inspect each item for its complete source references.

The broader programme

The later analysis covers reflective human development, trustworthy institutions, the Laboratory, Forge/model adaptation, Vocal Presence, federation, material and energy services, and enforceable security. It separates philosophical hypotheses from institutional mechanisms and distinguishes local success from a durable shift in institutional selection. Those branches are now in the map as proposed changes with their risks, first steps and actions. Most of the first steps are the analysis’s own planning, so each obstacle carries a question to check at a host before relying on it.

What counts as progress?

The earlier analysis proposes measuring practices adopted and retained in viable institutions that remain faithful to their purpose. The measures, system boundary, observation period and protected conditions still need agreement. Essays and conversation alone do not establish that outcome.

What “accepted for now” means

Named people are willing to build on a statement or connection for now. It records their judgement at the time. It does not establish truth, and it does not mean everyone agrees: each item names exactly who accepted it.

Contributed is not the same as accepted

Each item records who contributed the idea and, separately, who accepted it. Ideas suggested by the AI are attributed to the AI, and only people can accept anything.

What isn't here

The original forum posts, manuscript and source documents are not reproduced here. No participant endorsement, trial owner, budget, baseline, numerical target, feedback commitment or observed result is recorded. The map does not establish one global constraint.

A snapshot, not a live feed

This is release 2026-10-23.2, published 23 October 2026. Earlier wording stays on the Changes page.
